## 2. Scope and Non-Mutation Boundary

This is the Phase 1 read-only evidence inventory and owner-routing result for the 31 admitted rows whose live `CharacterID` is exactly `PendingOwnerDecision`. It includes all eleven admitted Realm batches, supplemental Sweden for comparison only, and the narrowly required live Project Bible identity, owner, Registry, household, Opening State, Network, and currentness authorities. No prompt, script, temporary file, log, separate receipt, or extracted fragment was written inside the Project Bible. No source Register, Registry, Opening State, Network, importer/payload, runtime, save, migration, or Unreal content was changed. Every Phase 1 planning record has `MutationEligibleInLaterTranche: false` and `RegistryPublicationEligible: false`.

## 4. Structural Baseline Reproduction

The complete Tranche 4 deterministic validator was rerun against the live Project Bible and passed: `fences=181`, `rows=133`, `fields=43`, `controlled_deviations=0`, `yaml_errors=0`, `duplicate_keys=0`, `admitted_ids=127`, `distinct=127`, `collisions=0`, `England=30/30`, `Wales=14/14`, `Normandy NMD=15`, `Normandy NOR=0`, `Norway NOR=20`, and `pending_sentinels=31`. Supplemental Sweden yielded 6 rows and was excluded from admitted counts.

Validator command: `python "C:\\Users\\thetr\\.copilot\\session-state\\26a2f1fa-aa52-4e5f-be4f-5f20a64ce0b3\\files\\Tranche4_validator.py"`. The validator uses duplicate-key-rejecting PyYAML loading and the 43 ordered fields enumerated in §5. No baseline drift or controlled-value deviation was found.

## 5. Deterministic Extraction Method

Both extraction passes used the same live-file method. Python `re` selected YAML fenced blocks matching `(?ims)^```ya?ml[ \\t]*\\r?\\n(.*?)^```[ \\t]*\\r?$`; PyYAML `SafeLoader` with a custom mapping constructor rejected duplicate keys. Every YAML document was walked recursively for mappings containing both `RegisterRecordID` and `CharacterID`. Exactly the 11 admitted batch files were filtered for `CharacterID == "PendingOwnerDecision"`; Sweden was parsed separately and excluded. Each selected mapping was asserted to contain exactly the following 43 fields in this order:

```text
RegisterRecordID RecordKind CharacterID DisplayName DisambiguationLabel RealmContext CanonicalOwner PopulationChannel PrimaryImplementationFamily FunctionalSubtype SecondaryInterfaces LifecycleState OpeningParticipation SimulationTier MapEmbodiment Controllability CommandEligibility GovernmentEligibility AdmiralEligibility AgentEligibility OfficeEligibility MarriageExecutionEligibility IndependentDiplomacyEligibility TitleExecutionEligibility HouseholdRoles DynastyRoles OfficeBindings CommandBindings TitleAndClaimInterfaces RelationshipInterfaces InstitutionalInterfaces HistoricalEmergenceInterface TraitInputReferences TraitExclusions PlayerCardPresentationProfile AIInterpretationProfile FieldResolutionSummary SourceAuthorities ExternalOwnerDependencies UnresolvedFields ProhibitedAssumptions ValidationStatus Notes
```

Source-row hash method: serialize the complete ordered row mapping with `yaml.dump(row, Dumper=NoAliasSafeDumper, sort_keys=False, allow_unicode=True, default_flow_style=False, width=100000, line_break="\\n")`; remove trailing LF characters and append exactly one LF; hash the UTF-8 bytes with SHA-256. Markdown fences and surrounding prose are excluded. The immutable source-row evidence block is this canonical serialization; values, nested values, field names, and order are preserved. `NoAliasSafeDumper.ignore_aliases` returns true. Reproducible extraction logic: reject duplicate mapping keys; iterate the 12 batch paths in the manifest; parse every YAML fence; recursively collect canonical rows; assert the 43-field order; exclude Sweden; select admitted `CharacterID` exactly equal to the sentinel; serialize/hash each row as stated; preserve live source order.

**Accepted/published canonical CharacterIDs found for these 31 sentinel rows: 0.** The Scotland Registry lists `Character.Scotland.Ingibjorg_Finnsdottir` in its person table, but its detailed current-person registry records it as `RetainedCanonicalCandidate`, not an accepted canonical publication. Norway’s current map instead uses `Character.Scotland.IngibjorgFinnsdottir`. Both spellings are retained in §13 as unresolved candidate evidence; neither is selected as final. Normandy William/Matilda and Denmark targets below are explicitly pending candidates/reservations, not accepted canonical IDs.

## 13. Norway and Scotland Ingibjorg Subcase

- **Norway row:** `NOR-E01`, `ExternalPersonReferenceRow`. It declares Scotland as external owner, prohibits Norway-local activation/mutable-state copy, and requires Registry/migration and Network-owner decisions.
- **Scotland row:** `SCO-P02` is the admitted Scotland owner-side row, with `Character.Scotland.Ingibjorg_Finnsdottir` in its current CharacterID field. The Scotland ID Migration Registry lists this spelling and records detailed status `RetainedCanonicalCandidate`; it is not treated as an accepted publication. Its direct alias map includes `Ingibjorg_Finnsdottir` and display alias `Ingibiorg Finnsdottir`.
- **Norway evidence:** current Norway README V1.7 identifies the V1.2 migration map as current and repeats `Character.Scotland.IngibjorgFinnsdottir`. Norway map §7.7 lists that as `canonical_id` and aliases `Character.Norway.IngibjorgFinnsdottir`, `Character.Orkney.IngibjorgFinnsdottir`, `Character.Scotland.IngibiorgFinnsdottir`, `Character.Norway.IngibiorgFinnsdottir`, and `CHR_CROSS_INGIBJORG`. The map states Network membership does not create duplicate identity, active relationship, opening state, household, office, location, knowledge, or control.
- **Identity/ownership:** Scotland character authority supports a single cross-Realm person and forms `Ingibjorg Finnsdottir` / `Ingibjǫrg Finnsdóttir`. Parentage, marriage, residence, titles, and chronology are corroborating context, not ID evidence. Both paths claim Scotland ownership; no ownership transfer is indicated.
- **Decision required:** Scotland Registry/Character Foundation/Historical Uncertainty owners and Norway Registry/migration/Network owners must reconcile exact ID, status, reverse-owner references, and current package lock. The two spellings normalize to one diagnostic key, but normalization is not approval. No ID is selected; no Norway duplicate or active Network edge is created.

## 17. Owner-Routing Queue

Queues intentionally overlap because an external row may also be underage, Network-gated, or source-currentness-gated. Bryan Ronga is a final project-owner escalation only for NMD-E01/NMD-E02, where no narrower identity owner is named; this is not canonical ownership evidence.

### 1. Single-Realm confirmation

Rows (15): `FLA-R-BALDWIN-V`, `FLA-R-ADELA`, `FLA-R-ROBERT`, `denmark.person.sweyn_estridsen`, `denmark.person.harald_sweynson`, `denmark.person.canute_sweynson`, `denmark.person.eric_sweynson`, `denmark.person.sigrid_sweynsdaughter`, `denmark.person.william_roskilde`, `denmark.history.ulf_jarl`, `denmark.history.estrid_svendsdatter`, `H-NL-001-DIRK-V`, `H-NL-002-GERTRUDE`, `H-NL-003-BERTHA`, `H-NL-004-FLORIS-YOUNGER`.

Owner route: Registry owner plus owning Realm Character Authority; add household, Opening State, Network, or historical owner by row.

### 2. External canonical-owner response required

Rows (9): `FLA-X-BALDWIN-OF-MONS`, `FLA-X-RICHILDE`, `FLA-X-MATILDA`, `FLA-X-WILLIAM-NORMANDY`, `FLA-X-PHILIP-I`, `FLA-X-ANNE-KIEV`, `denmark.external.adalbert_hamburg_bremen`, `denmark.external.gottschalk`, `H-NL-005-ROBERT-EXTERNAL`.
